独立评测发现

来源：独立评审 · 口径 v2 · 源 Run 38643271089 / attempt 1

最新上游状态：未复核。原评审“已解决”不作为上游修复证明；应用绕行、业务 QA 通过均不关闭框架问题。

等级与类型怎么定
  • 严重阻塞交付，或造成数据丢失、安全问题，且没有合理绕行
  • 重要按指引或公开 API 正常使用会静默得到错误结果，或只能用非公开手段绕行
  • 轻微能完成，但要多花排查成本或在应用侧绕行
  • 信息措辞、示例完整度等不影响结果的改进
  • 实现缺陷实现违背文档或公开约定
  • 能力缺口需求属于框架职责，但缺少能力或公开接入
  • 指引错误指引、示例或注释写错，或彼此矛盾，照做会出错
  • 指引缺失指引缺少必要说明或示例，需要读源码或试探
  • 易用性改进现有能力可用，但有改进空间

F4 · 调度 Skill/参考缺少可直接复用的启用/停用 UI 接线示例

文档 / Skill / 示例 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

原始证据支持这条指引缺口：E8/E9 证实 scheduler Skill 与 operations.md 只给路由、权限与错误码，要求应用自建可查看/启停的管理页，却没有任何客户端接线示例；E7 证实插件已提供 enable/disable 路由并返回更新后的 Schedule；E25/E26/E31 证实首轮只做只读页、修复才补上 actions.tsx。结论是 guidance-gap（指引完整度），不是契约违约或运行时缺陷。合理替代解释不成立：应用开发 Skill 已有通用 useApiClient+写请求范例（E55），但 scheduler 参考既未引用也未给出本插件路由的最小示例，通用范例不能替代专属接线；环境/工厂因素（APP_SAMPLE_DATA、样例种子）解释的是空待办与 B01/B02，与缺少启停示例无因果关系。

  • 职责与公开约定：operations.md 承诺 list/detail/occurrences/enable/disable 路由、page:scheduler.schedules/access 权限与 403/404/400 错误码，并明确插件不贡献页面、由应用自建；SKILL.md 要求管理员可查看并启用/停用。框架职责边界是提供 API 与路由，页面接线归应用，故建议补最小示例不构成违约。 E8 E9
  • 实际行为证据：插件路由确实实现 POST /scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/enable|disable 并返回更新后的 Schedule；两处冻结参考文本只有路由/权限/错误码表，没有组件或 useApiClient 调用示例；修复后的应用 actions.tsx 按 enabled 调 enable/disable 并用返回值就地更新。 E7 E8 E31
  • 应用接入与业务边界：首轮应用只实现 GET 列表/详情与执行记录，浏览器里没有任何启停控件，B03 因此 failed；retro 明确根因是自建页面未接插件已提供的 POST enable/disable，并在修复轮新增 actions.tsx。 E25 E26 E31
  • 环境因素：APP_SAMPLE_DATA/样例种子只解释 /main/todos 为空与 B01/B02 无法验证；E26 把“空待办”和“只读计划页”拆成两个 blocker，缺少启停入口不是外部服务、凭据或安装问题，环境因素不能解释该指引缺口。 E25 E26
  • 工厂因素：缺口位于插件 skill/reference 正文本身，retro 将其归为 skills-docs 改进；冻结材料中没有工厂提示、注入配置或 QA 脚本要求跳过启停接线，故不是工厂覆盖造成。 E8 E9 E26
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：应用开发 Skill 已文档化 useApiClient + api.request(POST/PATCH) 并从返回 {data} 取值的通用写模式，插件路由也已存在，能力并非缺失；但 scheduler 参考未给出本路由的最小示例或指向该通用模式，Agent 需自行从 dist/类型推断，改进空间成立。 E55 E15 E31

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

operations.md 完整给出 list/detail/occurrences/enable/disable 路由、权限与错误码，SKILL.md 也要求管理员可查看/启停，但两者都没有最小可抄的客户端示例（组件、请求路径与就地更新）。首轮实现据此只做了只读页。

实际影响
Agent 需从 dist 或类型自行推断启停接线，增加返工风险；属指引完整度的小缺口，不阻塞。
建议改法
在 references/operations.md 或 SKILL.md 增加 enable/disable 的最小客户端示例（useApiClient 调 POST /scheduler/schedules/:id/enable|disable 并替换返回的 Schedule）。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
Agent 仅凭 SKILL.md 概览构建管理页。
应有能力 / 行为
指引提供启停 UI 的最小接线示例。
实际观察
SKILL.md/operations.md 只有路由与权限表，无客户端示例。
应用绕行与代价
应用自行从插件 dist/类型推断并实现。
改进后的验收标准
按新示例可实现启停且刷新后状态保持。
评审时证据 · 冻结快照 3 条
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:9–27E8

管理入口表列出 list/detail/occurrences/enable/disable 路由、所需 page 权限与错误码，并声明插件不提供页面。

## Management Entry Points

The plugin contributes no pages: the application builds its schedule list and detail pages on the routes below and declares them among its own routes, guarded by the same permission. Definitions are maintained in source. The API supports viewing and enabling/disabling tasks, with no route for creating or editing Cron definitions.

All routes below require authentication and authorization for `{ resource: { type: 'page', id: 'scheduler.schedules' }, action: 'access' }`, checked before the schedule is looked up. Enabling/disabling currently uses the same permission, not a separate update action.

| Method and path                                        | Response / effect                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules`                         | `{ data: ScheduleListItem[], meta: { page, pageSize, total } }`; `page` (default 1) and `pageSize` (default 20, at most 100)                                       |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId`             | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/occurrences` | `{ data: ScheduleOccurrenceView[], meta: { nextPageToken? } }`, newest first; `pageSize` (default 20, at most 100) and `pageToken` from the previous page's `meta` |
| `POST /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/enable`     | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`; enables the task, no request body                                                                                                    |
| `POST /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/disable`    | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`; pauses the task, no request body                                                                                                     |

Failures use the standard `/api` error body with domain `scheduler`: a caller without page access gets `403` with reason `SCHEDULE_ACCESS_REQUIRED`, an id this application has no schedule for gets `404` with reason `SCHEDULE_NOT_FOUND`, and an invalid `page`, `pageSize` or `pageToken` gets `400` with reason `INVALID_INPUT`. Treat `pageToken` as opaque; the last page has no `nextPageToken`.

The running application documents these routes under the `Scheduler` tag at `/api/swagger/docs` (JSON at `/api/swagger`, signed in), with the full `ScheduleListItem` and occurrence schemas; their operationIds are `schedulerListSchedules`, `schedulerGetSchedule`, `schedulerListOccurrences`, `schedulerEnableSchedule` and `schedulerDisableSchedule`.

Use the id returned by list for `:scheduleId`, not the definition key or a target config value. Enabling/disabling affects future scheduling; it does not cancel work already started.
app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:12–46E9

Skill 要求管理员界面可观察、可启用/停用，并指向 references/operations.md 作为只读入口。

## Decide Whether to Use Scheduler

The primary reason to define tasks through Scheduler is **UI observability for business administrators**. Developers define schedules and execution logic in code. Business administrators use the task list and detail pages the application builds on the Scheduler API to view schedules, track individual executions, and enable or disable tasks when authorized.

Tasks that do not need to be viewed and tracked through the UI must not be managed by the Scheduler plugin. Give recurring ones a `ScheduleExecutor` of their own from the application's jobs service (`jobExecutorServiceToken` in `@nocobase/app-server/jobs`), and one-off background work a `JobExecutor`; the application development Skill describes both. For administrator-visible tasks, deliver Client registration, access permissions, meaningful task names, and accurate final execution status together; successful server-side dispatch alone is incomplete.

## Then Choose the Execution Model

After deciding to use Scheduler, choose according to business complexity:

| Scenario                                                                                  | Choice          | Implementation boundary                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Periodic cleanup, cache refresh, a single report, or one business Service call            | Own target type | Short operations may complete directly; hand lengthy work to a `JobExecutor`                             |
| A simple operation that happens hourly                                                    | Own target type | Cron alone is not a reason to introduce a heavier execution system                                       |
| Immediate asynchronous execution                                                          | `JobExecutor`   | No Cron Schedule is needed                                                                               |
| A one-time delay, such as ten minutes after a request                                     | Queue `delay`   | No Cron Schedule is needed; `JobExecutor` has no delay                                                   |
| Another execution system with its own references, status queries, and completion protocol | Own target type | The same `registerTarget()` call, with `inspect()` and completion reporting for work that finishes later |

Every schedule points at a registered target; `registerTarget()` is the target extension surface, and there is no built-in target type. A target does not guarantee exactly-once external business effects; design business idempotency.

## Read by Task

- To create or change a schedule, read [Definitions, Registration, and Synchronization](references/definitions.md).
- To register or review a target, hand its work to a `JobExecutor`, report completion, or diagnose a historical occurrence after retargeting, read [Target Extensions and Execution Protocol](references/targets.md).
- To integrate or review the UI/API and permissions, interpret status, or diagnose missing definitions, read [Operations and Verification](references/operations.md), including the complete management route table, separate status dimensions, and application identity checks.

## Development Loop

1. Establish which tasks and execution records administrators need to see. Check Server/Client/CLI registration, page permissions, the application's `jobs` configuration (a `redis` adapter for more than one instance; `memory` serves one process) and `JobExecutorServiceProvider` composition and target availability, then choose or register the target type.
2. Implement business logic and Providers in application source, and call `schedulerServiceToken.defineSchedule(definition)` with an application-wide stable key from that Provider's `register()`/`boot()`.
3. Validate payload/input, timezone, idempotency, and the full asynchronous chain: submission, actual job execution, terminal notification, and recovery from persisted execution state. Obtain credentials through secure business Service configuration, never `target.config`.
4. Run application type checks, relevant tests, and build. Synchronize definitions and, in development, use an administrator account to find the task in the UI and track a real execution to its final state. Confirm the business result.
5. Report the schedule key, execution model, timezone, validation evidence, and unverified runtime boundaries. Use `--finalize` in production only when the complete manifest is visible.

Schedules are defined in code. There is no management API for creating or editing Cron definitions, but the UI and API support enabling and disabling tasks. Do not modify `schedule_definitions`, `schedule_occurrences`, or the schedule backend's rules (BullMQ job schedulers, or the memory adapter's state file) directly, and do not add jobs under Scheduler's `@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler` scope.

### [信息][指引缺失] 调度 Skill/参考缺少可直接复用的启用/停用 UI 接线示例

**相关对象**：`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md`、`app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md`

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

原始证据支持这条指引缺口：E8/E9 证实 scheduler Skill 与 operations.md 只给路由、权限与错误码，要求应用自建可查看/启停的管理页，却没有任何客户端接线示例；E7 证实插件已提供 enable/disable 路由并返回更新后的 Schedule；E25/E26/E31 证实首轮只做只读页、修复才补上 actions.tsx。结论是 guidance-gap（指引完整度），不是契约违约或运行时缺陷。合理替代解释不成立：应用开发 Skill 已有通用 useApiClient+写请求范例（E55），但 scheduler 参考既未引用也未给出本插件路由的最小示例，通用范例不能替代专属接线；环境/工厂因素（APP_SAMPLE_DATA、样例种子）解释的是空待办与 B01/B02，与缺少启停示例无因果关系。

- 职责与公开约定：operations.md 承诺 list/detail/occurrences/enable/disable 路由、page:scheduler.schedules/access 权限与 403/404/400 错误码，并明确插件不贡献页面、由应用自建；SKILL.md 要求管理员可查看并启用/停用。框架职责边界是提供 API 与路由，页面接线归应用，故建议补最小示例不构成违约。；证据：E8（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:9–27）、E9（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:12–46）
- 实际行为证据：插件路由确实实现 POST /scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/enable|disable 并返回更新后的 Schedule；两处冻结参考文本只有路由/权限/错误码表，没有组件或 useApiClient 调用示例；修复后的应用 actions.tsx 按 enabled 调 enable/disable 并用返回值就地更新。；证据：E7（packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/routes/index.js:113–144）、E8（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:9–27）、E31（app/client/pages/scheduler/actions.tsx:48–72）
- 应用接入与业务边界：首轮应用只实现 GET 列表/详情与执行记录，浏览器里没有任何启停控件，B03 因此 failed；retro 明确根因是自建页面未接插件已提供的 POST enable/disable，并在修复轮新增 actions.tsx。；证据：E25（artifacts/verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json:1–76）、E26（artifacts/retro.json:4–37）、E31（app/client/pages/scheduler/actions.tsx:48–72）
- 环境因素：APP_SAMPLE_DATA/样例种子只解释 /main/todos 为空与 B01/B02 无法验证；E26 把“空待办”和“只读计划页”拆成两个 blocker，缺少启停入口不是外部服务、凭据或安装问题，环境因素不能解释该指引缺口。；证据：E25（artifacts/verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json:1–76）、E26（artifacts/retro.json:4–37）
- 工厂因素：缺口位于插件 skill/reference 正文本身，retro 将其归为 skills-docs 改进；冻结材料中没有工厂提示、注入配置或 QA 脚本要求跳过启停接线，故不是工厂覆盖造成。；证据：E8（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:9–27）、E9（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:12–46）、E26（artifacts/retro.json:4–37）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：应用开发 Skill 已文档化 useApiClient + api.request(POST/PATCH) 并从返回 {data} 取值的通用写模式，插件路由也已存在，能力并非缺失；但 scheduler 参考未给出本路由的最小示例或指向该通用模式，Agent 需自行从 dist/类型推断，改进空间成立。；证据：E55（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/api.md:27–53）、E15（packages/@nocobase/app-client/dist/hooks.js:11–16）、E31（app/client/pages/scheduler/actions.tsx:48–72）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

operations.md 完整给出 list/detail/occurrences/enable/disable 路由、权限与错误码，SKILL.md 也要求管理员可查看/启停，但两者都没有最小可抄的客户端示例（组件、请求路径与就地更新）。首轮实现据此只做了只读页。

**影响**：Agent 需从 dist 或类型自行推断启停接线，增加返工风险；属指引完整度的小缺口，不阻塞。

**建议改动**：在 references/operations.md 或 SKILL.md 增加 enable/disable 的最小客户端示例（useApiClient 调 POST /scheduler/schedules/:id/enable|disable 并替换返回的 Schedule）。

**验收标准**：按新示例可实现启停且刷新后状态保持。

- `app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:12–46` — Skill 要求管理员界面可观察、可启用/停用，并指向 references/operations.md 作为只读入口。
- `artifacts/verify-1/browser-acceptance/report.json:1–76` — verify-1 passed=false：3 条待办缺失且无停用入口，B01/B02/B03 failed。

F5 · 各发布包 dist 布局不一致，增加 Agent 类型/入口探索成本

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/repository-input · 原评审：未解决

⌄

反馈证据核对

核心事实成立：E58/E16/E59 显示 @nocobase/db 的公开入口物理位置为 dist/src/index.js，而 @nocobase/repository-input 与 @nocobase/app-client 为扁平 dist/index.js，app-testing 亦用 dist/src；E36 记录按 dist/src 猜测 repository-input 类型失败，E41 记录直接枚举 @nocobase/db-testing/dist 不存在，均属实际探索成本。E57/E28 的 exports 映射保证解析正确，E56 已要求只经文档化 exports，未发现运行或装配错误，故属 usability-improvement。需说明：候选所列 E45 实际是 sqlite 逻辑列名/物理列名查询差异，与包 dist 布局无关，本次未将其作为依据。替代解释：pnpm 顶层软链与 .pnpm 嵌套（E37）部分解释 db-testing 不在顶层，但不能消除布局不一致造成的直读失败。

  • 职责与公开约定：公开入口布局确有差异：db 为 dist/src/index.js，repository-input 与 app-client 为扁平 dist/index.js；包 exports 才是官方解析契约，应用开发 Skill 也要求只走文档化 exports、不得导入插件内部源码路径。没有任何冻结约定承诺各包物理 dist 目录必须一致，故这是易用性偏好而非违约。 E16 E58 E59 E56
  • 实际行为证据：日志证据显示 Agent 按 dist/src 读取 repository-input 的 .d.ts 失败（退出码 2），且 @nocobase/db-testing/dist 在顶层不存在；物理布局差异与各包 package.json 的 main/types 声明一致。E45 是 sqlite 列名问题，不支持本条，未采用。 E36 E41 E16 E28
  • 应用接入与业务边界：探索失败源于按物理路径猜测，而非应用错误装配：app-client 首次 find 为空后改读 exports/软链即成功；最终 verify-2 全部通过，未产生业务或运行缺陷。 E36 E37 E41
  • 环境因素：pnpm 顶层软链与 .pnpm 嵌套、各包既有 exports 映射，解释了 db-testing 不在顶层及部分直读失败；这是包发布/安装布局而非外部服务或凭据问题，且不影响按 exports 正确解析。 E37 E35
  • 工厂因素：快照内可见的工厂材料只有 app/factory-template.json，仅固定模板与插件版本，未定义或覆盖各包 dist 布局；没有工厂 prompt/构建脚本原文可证明其要求直读物理路径，故该不一致保留为上游打包约定。 E27
  • 已有能力与推荐用法：各包 exports 已能正确解析，应用开发 Skill 也明确要求经 documented package exports 访问；Agent 原本无需直读物理路径，改进点是统一输出布局或在 Skill 中显式说明入口，而非能力缺口。 E56 E28 E16

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

@nocobase/db 的公开入口为 dist/src/index.js，而 @nocobase/repository-input 与 @nocobase/app-client 为扁平 dist/index.js。Agent 在实现期多次按 dist/src 猜测失败，并耗费步骤枚举 db-testing/app-testing 的 dist。exports 映射保证解析正确，但对按物理路径直读的 Agent 不友好。

实际影响
仅增加探索成本，不影响运行结果。
建议改法
统一包内 dist 目录约定，或在 Skill 中说明应通过 package exports 解析而非猜测物理路径。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
Agent 直接按物理路径读取 node_modules 中包的类型/入口。
应有能力 / 行为
同一模板内包布局一致或明确文档化。
实际观察
db 用 dist/src，repository-input/app-client 用扁平 dist；直读失败。
应用绕行与代价
改读 exports 映射或逐包枚举。
改进后的验收标准
同一模板的包使用一致 dist 布局，或 Skill 明确给出通过 exports 解析的说明。

上游 Issue 草稿 · Markdown
### [信息][易用性改进] 各发布包 dist 布局不一致，增加 Agent 类型/入口探索成本

**相关对象**：`@nocobase/repository-input`（评审时 0.1.0-beta.2）

**反馈证据核对**：反馈复核：证据支持

核心事实成立：E58/E16/E59 显示 @nocobase/db 的公开入口物理位置为 dist/src/index.js，而 @nocobase/repository-input 与 @nocobase/app-client 为扁平 dist/index.js，app-testing 亦用 dist/src；E36 记录按 dist/src 猜测 repository-input 类型失败，E41 记录直接枚举 @nocobase/db-testing/dist 不存在，均属实际探索成本。E57/E28 的 exports 映射保证解析正确，E56 已要求只经文档化 exports，未发现运行或装配错误，故属 usability-improvement。需说明：候选所列 E45 实际是 sqlite 逻辑列名/物理列名查询差异，与包 dist 布局无关，本次未将其作为依据。替代解释：pnpm 顶层软链与 .pnpm 嵌套（E37）部分解释 db-testing 不在顶层，但不能消除布局不一致造成的直读失败。

- 职责与公开约定：公开入口布局确有差异：db 为 dist/src/index.js，repository-input 与 app-client 为扁平 dist/index.js；包 exports 才是官方解析契约，应用开发 Skill 也要求只走文档化 exports、不得导入插件内部源码路径。没有任何冻结约定承诺各包物理 dist 目录必须一致，故这是易用性偏好而非违约。；证据：E16（packages/@nocobase/repository-input/dist/index.js:1–2）、E58（packages/@nocobase/db/dist/src/index.js:1–2）、E59（packages/@nocobase/app-client/dist/index.js:1–6）、E56（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/SKILL.md:162–162）
- 实际行为证据：日志证据显示 Agent 按 dist/src 读取 repository-input 的 .d.ts 失败（退出码 2），且 @nocobase/db-testing/dist 在顶层不存在；物理布局差异与各包 package.json 的 main/types 声明一致。E45 是 sqlite 列名问题，不支持本条，未采用。；证据：E36（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0009.txt:1217–1221）、E41（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0033.txt:2256–2260）、E16（packages/@nocobase/repository-input/dist/index.js:1–2）、E28（packages/@nocobase/repository-input/package.json:1–14）
- 应用接入与业务边界：探索失败源于按物理路径猜测，而非应用错误装配：app-client 首次 find 为空后改读 exports/软链即成功；最终 verify-2 全部通过，未产生业务或运行缺陷。；证据：E36（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0009.txt:1217–1221）、E37（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0012.txt:1132–1136）、E41（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0033.txt:2256–2260）
- 环境因素：pnpm 顶层软链与 .pnpm 嵌套、各包既有 exports 映射，解释了 db-testing 不在顶层及部分直读失败；这是包发布/安装布局而非外部服务或凭据问题，且不影响按 exports 正确解析。；证据：E37（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0012.txt:1132–1136）、E35（artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0007.txt:251–255）
- 工厂因素：快照内可见的工厂材料只有 app/factory-template.json，仅固定模板与插件版本，未定义或覆盖各包 dist 布局；没有工厂 prompt/构建脚本原文可证明其要求直读物理路径，故该不一致保留为上游打包约定。；证据：E27（app/factory-template.json:1–76）
- 已有能力与推荐用法：各包 exports 已能正确解析，应用开发 Skill 也明确要求经 documented package exports 访问；Agent 原本无需直读物理路径，改进点是统一输出布局或在 Skill 中显式说明入口，而非能力缺口。；证据：E56（app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/SKILL.md:162–162）、E28（packages/@nocobase/repository-input/package.json:1–14）、E16（packages/@nocobase/repository-input/dist/index.js:1–2）

反馈复核是对原始反馈的证据核对，不等于人工确认、上游修复状态或自动修复授权；保留原评审置信度、分类与处理状态。

@nocobase/db 的公开入口为 dist/src/index.js，而 @nocobase/repository-input 与 @nocobase/app-client 为扁平 dist/index.js。Agent 在实现期多次按 dist/src 猜测失败，并耗费步骤枚举 db-testing/app-testing 的 dist。exports 映射保证解析正确，但对按物理路径直读的 Agent 不友好。

**影响**：仅增加探索成本，不影响运行结果。

**建议改动**：统一包内 dist 目录约定，或在 Skill 中说明应通过 package exports 解析而非猜测物理路径。

**验收标准**：同一模板的包使用一致 dist 布局，或 Skill 明确给出通过 exports 解析的说明。

**评审时证据（冻结快照）**：
- `packages/@nocobase/repository-input/dist/index.js:1–2` — repository-input 公开入口直接导出 buildFilter/buildSelect/buildCreateValues，物理入口为扁平 dist/index.js。
- `packages/@nocobase/repository-input/package.json:1–14` — repository-input 包公开入口为 ./dist/index.js 与 ./dist/index.d.ts（扁平 dist）。
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0009.txt:1217–1221` — 按 dist/src 猜测 repository-input 类型路径失败，实际为扁平 dist。
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0033.txt:2256–2260` — 查找 @nocobase/db-testing/dist 不存在，属包布局探索。
- `artifacts/agent-history/agent-implement.jsonl/part-0040.txt:962–966` — sqlite3 以逻辑名 dueAt 查询报 no such column，随后改用物理列名。

> 来源：nb3-factory 独立搭建评审（口径 v2，源 Run 38643271089 / attempt 1）。尚未复核最新上游源码。

F2 · 工厂安装/验收流程未设置 APP_SAMPLE_DATA，示例种子被静默跳过并导致首轮 B01/B02 失败

工厂流程 · @nocobase/db · @nocobase/app-server · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md · 原评审：未解决

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

任务元数据声明示例数据=是（E50 前的任务上下文/元数据），应用首版将夹具写成 sample:true 的 seed。框架语义要求 sample seed 仅在首次安装且 app.sampleData=true 时运行，否则记录 skipped（E10/E11/E13）。修复期 grep 确认 .github 脚本没有任何阶段设置 APP_SAMPLE_DATA（E50），verify-1 数据库 todos count=0 且 seed history 记录该 sample seed 未运行（E53）。

实际影响
验收所需 3 条待办不可见，B01/B02 首轮失败，Agent 需额外一轮把夹具改为必需 seed，增加一次工厂验收。
建议改法
工厂在安装/验收前导出 APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true 或显式执行一次 db sample，并在任务元数据 sampleData=是 时断言示例数据已加载。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
任务元数据 sampleData=是，应用使用 defineSeed({sample:true})，而工厂 apply-database/verify/browser-acceptance 脚本不设置 APP_SAMPLE_DATA。
应有能力 / 行为
示例数据在验收环境可用，或工厂在验收前显式启用示例数据并在日志中说明。
实际观察
sample seed 被记录为 skipped，todos 表为空（count=0），B01/B02 首轮失败。
应用绕行与代价
应用把种子改为必需 seed 202610070001_todos.ts，不再依赖示例数据开关。
改进后的验收标准
在 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 未设置时，工厂验收前断言示例种子已加载或明确输出示例数据未启用；B01/B02 无需额外修复即可判定。

F3 · 首版调度管理页只读、缺少启用/停用入口，导致 B03 首轮失败

Agent / 业务实现 · @nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md

⌄

未提供反馈复核记录，保留为候选反馈；原评审置信度不代表已排除误报。

插件的启停路由与 page 权限门禁齐备，operations.md 明确列出启停入口。首版应用只实现读取与展示，verify-1 记录浏览器可达 UI 没有任何停用入口；修复轮 actions.tsx 接入 enable/disable 后 B03 通过。

实际影响
B03 首轮失败并消耗一次修复轮；与框架能力无关。
建议改法
应用按 operations.md 路由表实现启停控件，并复用插件返回体就地更新状态。
诊断 · 触发条件、应有行为、绕行代价与验收标准
触发条件
首版应用仅调用 list/detail 只读路由。
应有能力 / 行为
管理页提供启用/停用并在成功后持久化。
实际观察
verify-1 页面无停用入口，B03 failed。
应用绕行与代价
修复轮新增 actions.tsx，按 enabled 调 POST enable|disable。
改进后的验收标准
在设置页停用计划并刷新后仍为停用，B03 通过。
E7 E8 E31 E25 E24
实现者过程与运行背景 · 2 个过程问题 / 2 条建议 · 不自动归为框架问题

本任务已处理只描述应用侧处理结果，不代表 NocoBase3 上游已修复。

本轮过程记录

浏览器验收暴露三处缺陷：全新安装的数据库没有待办数据（示例 Seed 在没有 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 的环境里从未执行），以及 Scheduler 页面只能看不能启停计划。把待办 Seed 提升为必需幂等 Seed，并给计划列表与详情补上启用/停用（停用前用 AlertDialog 确认），同时补齐自测与文档。

问题 01 · 浏览器验收

/main/todos 空列表：示例 Seed 在新装环境从未执行

处理结果未提供

全新安装后 todos 页面 0 条记录，并显示“加载示例数据”的提示，但页面没有加载入口；Schedule 因此也无法验证重复执行不重复。

原因（实现者自述）
Seed 以 sample: true 声明，只有在 app.sampleData（APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true）时才运行；工厂脚本（db apply、verify、browser-acceptance）都不设置该变量，也不执行 db sample，于是它只被记录为 skipped。
处理方式
将 database/main/seeds/202610070001_sample_todos.ts 改为必需 Seed 202610070001_todos.ts（去掉 sample: true，按 title 幂等 upsert），db apply 即写入 1 条过期、1 条未来、1 条已完成待办；同步修正 todos 空态文案与 AGENTS.md，并新增 Seed 首装/重跑/不覆盖用户修改的测试。
影响 / 代价：先确认工厂脚本不会设置 APP_SAMPLE_DATA，再改名以避免与已记录 skipped 的历史冲突来源：Agent · retro.json
问题 02 · 浏览器验收

/main/scheduler 只读：计划无法启用/停用

处理结果未提供

计划列表与详情只显示状态徽章和“执行记录”链接，没有启停控件，也没有可管理计划的位置。

原因（实现者自述）
应用自建 Scheduler 页面时只实现了 GET 列表/详情与执行记录，没有接上插件已经提供的 POST /scheduler/schedules/:id/enable|disable。
处理方式
新增 client/pages/scheduler/actions.tsx：列表每行一个 icon-only ghost “…” 菜单（执行记录 + 启用/停用），详情页头部一个 outline 启停按钮；停用前用 AlertDialog（size='sm'、destructive 确认按钮）确认，成功后用接口返回的计划就地更新状态；补齐中英文案、AGENTS.md 与组件测试。
影响 / 代价：同一控件要同时支持菜单项与按钮两种形态，并处理受控 AlertDialog 的开关来源：Agent · retro.json

实现者补充建议

来自可选过程笔记，不代表已独立确认；相同建议引用上方评审，其余保留原文。

建议 01 · 脚手架默认值

工厂新装环境不会加载 sample Seed，任务元数据的“示例数据：是”不产生效果

可自动化

任务元数据带有 sampleData 字段，但 apply-database.sh / verify.sh / browser-acceptance.sh 都执行 pnpm nocobase db apply 且不设置 APP_SAMPLE_DATA，sample: true 的 Seed 只会记录 skipped。Agent 很容易按惯例写成 sample Seed，浏览器验收就看到空页面。

建议改动

在工厂安装/验收前设置 APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true（或执行一次 nocobase db sample），或在 scaffold 与 implement prompt 中明确“验收可见的数据必须用必需 Seed”，并提供带示例数据、幂等的模板 Seed。

来源：Agent · retro.json

建议 02 · Skill 与文档

应用内管理插件资源（如 Scheduler 计划启停）缺少可照抄的示例

需人工判断

Scheduler Skill 说明插件不提供页面，但没有给出应用自定义管理页接 enable/disable 的示例；这次需要读插件 dist 才确认 POST 路由与返回 { data: Schedule }。

建议改动

在 nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler 的 Skill 中补一个最小示例：列表行菜单 + 详情按钮 + AlertDialog 确认 + 用返回的计划就地更新状态。

来源：Agent · retro.json

@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler 服务端的 Schedule 定义校验、目标注册、cron 派发、Occurrence 幂等与 Provider 生命周期。

插件@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler1.0.0-beta.16

schedulerServiceToken · defineSchedule(definition) · registerTarget(target) · SchedulerProvider · ScheduleTargetRegistry

E1 E2 E3 E4 E5 E33

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

每分钟真实触发一次‘检查过期待办’并把结果记录为可查询的执行记录，而非直接改库伪造。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
插件负责 cron 解析/派发、目标 handler 调用与 occurrence 落库；业务标记规则由应用 handler 提供。
推荐使用方式
在应用 Provider 中用 schedulerServiceToken.defineSchedule 声明计划并用 registerTarget 绑定 handler。
Agent 实际使用
TodoSchedulerProvider 在 boot() 注册 app.todos.check-overdue 并 defineSchedule cron '* * * * *'，verify-2 真实触发。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E3 E17
重复触发同一目标不产生重复执行或冲突记录。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
插件以 occurrenceId（jobId）去重，重复投递返回 noop。
推荐使用方式
直接使用插件的 occurrence 生命周期，无需应用自行加锁。
Agent 实际使用
应用依赖插件幂等；B02 再执行一次待办总数不增加且状态正确。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口

E29

需求 → 框架职责 → 推荐与实际接入

用 Migration 建 todos 结构，并把验收所需的 1 条过期、1 条未来、1 条已完成待办写入数据库。直接支持
框架职责与业务边界
库提供 migration 建表与 seed 执行；具体数据属应用内容。
推荐使用方式
结构写 migration；应用运行需要的数据写普通 seed；纯示例内容写 sample seed。
Agent 实际使用
应用 migration 建表，seed 幂等写入 3 条待办，最终改为必需 seed 后 B01/B02 通过。
缺口归因
未记录能力缺口
E18 E19 E12 E30
任务元数据声明‘示例数据：是’时，示例数据应在验收环境可见。需要绕行
框架职责与业务边界
框架既定语义只在首次安装且 app.sampleData=true 时运行 sample seed，否则记录 skipped；工厂需在验收前声明或启用示例数据。
推荐使用方式
验收需要的数据用必需 seed；sample seed 仅作可选演示，并需环境设置 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 或 db sample 补跑。
Agent 实际使用
首版用 sample:true，工厂脚本不设 APP_SAMPLE_DATA，sample seed 被记录 skipped，verify-1 todos 为 0、B01/B02 首轮失败。
缺口归因

首轮与修复过程 · 查看逐轮原始状态
轮次范围原始结论失败项
验证 1全量浏览器 QA报告未通过B01 启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。
B02 再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。
B03 在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。 无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。
验证 2全量浏览器 QA报告通过未记录 failed 项；不代表全部操作完成

NocoBase3 库、插件、模板与指引 2

做得好的地方
F1 · Scheduler 插件的真实派发与 occurrence 幂等构成可靠核心能力

NocoBase3 插件 · @nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler

⌄

defineSchedule 前置校验 key/cron/时区并拒绝敏感配置；registerTarget 绑定应用 handler；派发以 jobId 作为 occurrenceId，先 start（重复返回 noop）再执行 handler 并按结果落状态；Provider 按 startupMode 订阅事件与对账。

提供的帮助
应用无需自建调度循环或去重锁，声明计划即可获得可查询执行记录；verify-2 的 B01/B02 真实验证了触发与幂等。
应保留的能力
保持 occurrence 与 jobId 的绑定；补充多实例与长任务场景的文档和测试。
E1 E3 E5 E33
做得好的地方
F6 · 迁移/种子的必需与示例分层语义正确且文档一致

NocoBase3 内核 · @nocobase/db · @nocobase/app-server · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md · app/AGENTS.md

⌄

DefaultSeeder 在 samplesEnabled 为假时把 sample seed 记为 skipped 而非执行，必需 seed 照常运行；app-server 在 freshInstall 时按 app.sampleData 决定示例种子开关；nocobase-db Skill 与根指引都明确区分必需数据与示例数据及跳过条件。

提供的帮助
框架提供了可靠、幂等的必需/示例数据分层，本次失败源于工厂未启用示例数据而非语义错误。
应保留的能力
在应用模板与工厂脚本之间做一致性校验，默认约定验收数据用必需 seed。
E10 E11 E12 E13 E29

问题、误导与建议统一收录在问题与改进，不在此重复展示。

本次评审的覆盖限制
  • 只读冻结快照，未运行应用、安装依赖或执行浏览器验收；UI 结论来自 verify-1/verify-2 归档截图。
  • 示例数据缺失的归因来自修复期 grep 与验收期 sqlite 读数，未直接读取快照外的 .github 工作流文件。
  • 未验证 Scheduler 多实例并发、长任务、时区边界与长期停用期间的触发行为。
  • 客户端单元仅评审 @nocobase/app-client 的容器/token/hooks，未评审应用自写的数据表格与 UI 组件质量。
  • 过程证据仅覆盖已归档的本 Run 调用，不代表 CLI 内部上下文或未暴露子 Agent 的完整阅读。
  • 快照未包含 2 个超出预算、非普通文件或不可读取的文件；未据此确认其实现。
评审基线与版本指纹

评审针对已封存候选补丁与当时的依赖；可能在原搭建内或之后单独补跑。与 PR 或 NocoBase3 当前最新提交不是同一概念。评审只读冻结材料，引用运行日志不代表评审者重新复现。此报告未执行最新上游源码复核。

E1 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/schedules/define.js:11–58

defineSchedule 对 key/title/cron/timezone/target 做前置校验，拒绝敏感配置并生成稳定 definitionHash。

export function defineSchedule(definition) {
    if (!definition.key || !/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]*$/.test(definition.key))
        throw new Error('Schedule key must be a stable identifier.');
    if (!definition.title)
        throw new Error('Schedule title is required.');
    const parts = definition.schedule?.cron?.trim().split(/\s+/) ?? [];
    if (parts.length !== 5 && parts.length !== 6)
        throw new Error('Schedule cron must contain five or six fields.');
    const timezone = definition.schedule.timezone ?? 'UTC';
    try {
        CronExpressionParser.parse(definition.schedule.cron, {
            currentDate: new Date('2020-01-01T00:00:00.000Z'),
            tz: timezone,
        });
    }
    catch {
        throw new Error('Schedule cron or timezone is invalid.');
    }
    if (!definition.target?.type)
        throw new Error('Schedule target type is required.');
    assertNoSensitiveConfig(definition.target.config);
    if (definition.schedule.limit !== undefined &&
        (!Number.isInteger(definition.schedule.limit) ||
            definition.schedule.limit < 1))
        throw new Error('Schedule limit must be a positive integer.');
    if (definition.schedule.from &&
        definition.schedule.to &&
        definition.schedule.from > definition.schedule.to)
        throw new Error('Schedule from must not be after to.');
    const normalized = {
        ...definition,
        schedule: {
            ...definition.schedule,
            cron: definition.schedule.cron.trim(),
            timezone,
        },
    };
    const canonical = stableStringify(normalized);
    return freezeDeep({
        ...normalized,
        definitionHash: createHash('sha256').update(canonical).digest('hex'),
    });
}
const SENSITIVE_CONFIG_KEY = /(?:apiKey|accessKey|accessToken|refreshToken|clientSecret|credential|credentials|password|privateKey|secret|token)$/i;
function assertNoSensitiveConfig(value, path = 'target.config') {
    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        value.forEach((child, index) => assertNoSensitiveConfig(child, `${path}[${index}]`));
        return;

E2 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/schedules/registry.js:3–48

ScheduleTargetRegistry 按 type 唯一注册，分发 validate/describe/start/inspect/referenceHref。

    register(target) {
        if (this.targets.has(target.type))
            throw new Error(`Schedule target type already registered: ${target.type}`);
        // The registry is heterogeneous: each target parses its own config out of
        // the JSON a definition declared, which `validate()` is there to check.
        this.targets.set(target.type, target);
    }
    get(type) {
        return this.targets.get(type);
    }
    validate(definition) {
        const target = this.get(definition.target.type);
        return target
            ? target.validate(definition.target.config)
            : { valid: false, reason: 'target-not-found' };
    }
    async describe(type, config) {
        const target = this.get(type);
        if (!target)
            return { targetLabel: type, state: 'missing' };
        return target.describe
            ? target.describe(config)
            : { targetLabel: target.title, state: 'ready' };
    }
    async start(type, config, context) {
        const target = this.get(type);
        if (!target)
            return { state: 'failed', reason: 'target-not-found' };
        const validation = target.validate(config);
        if (!validation.valid)
            return {
                state: 'failed',
                reason: validation.reason ?? 'invalid-config',
            };
        return target.start(config, context);
    }
    inspect(targetType, reference) {
        const target = this.get(targetType);
        return target?.inspect
            ? target.inspect(reference)
            : Promise.resolve({ state: 'unknown', reason: 'observer-unavailable' });
    }
    referenceHref(targetType, reference) {
        return this.get(targetType)?.referenceHref?.(reference);
    }
}

E3 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/dispatch.js:23–75

派发以 jobId 作为 occurrenceId：先 start（重复返回 noop）再执行 handler，按结果落 succeeded/skipped/failed。

async function dispatch(spec, context, { targets, occurrences }) {
    // The occurrence is the firing: its id is the executor's job id, so the same
    // firing delivered twice records, and starts its target, once.
    const occurrenceId = context.jobId;
    const executionContext = { scheduleId: spec.id, occurrenceId };
    const record = {
        scheduleId: spec.id,
        definitionHash: spec.definitionHash,
        targetType: spec.target.type,
        scheduledAt: context.scheduledAt,
    };
    const targetState = (await targets.describe(spec.target.type, spec.target.config)).state;
    if (targetState && targetState !== 'ready') {
        await occurrences.skip(occurrenceId, `target-${targetState}`, record);
        return;
    }
    const action = await occurrences.start(executionContext, spec.definitionHash, spec.target.type, context.scheduledAt);
    if (action === 'noop')
        return;
    try {
        const result = await targets.start(spec.target.type, spec.target.config, executionContext);
        switch (result.state) {
            case 'completed':
                await occurrences.succeed(occurrenceId, result.result);
                return;
            case 'accepted': {
                await occurrences.wait(occurrenceId, result.reference, result.receipt);
                const observation = await targets.inspect(spec.target.type, result.reference);
                if (observation.state === 'completed')
                    await occurrences.complete(occurrenceId, result.reference, observation.completion);
                return;
            }
            case 'skipped':
                await occurrences.skip(occurrenceId, result.reason);
                return;
            case 'failed':
                await occurrences.fail(occurrenceId, result.reason);
                return;
        }
    }
    catch (error) {
        try {
            await occurrences.fail(occurrenceId, 'dispatch-failed');
        }
        catch (completionError) {
            console.error('Failed to record schedule dispatch failure', {
                occurrenceId,
                error: completionError,
            });
        }
        throw error;
    }
}

E4 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/providers/scheduler.js:22–97

Provider register 只暴露一个合成 service；start 按 startupMode 订阅事件与安排对账。

    register() {
        // One service reaches the container. The target registry, the executor
        // and both stores are this provider's own parts, handed to whatever needs
        // them instead of being resolvable by anyone holding the container.
        this.app.container.singleton(schedulerServiceToken, () => this.compose().service);
    }
    async start() {
        const { executor, service } = this.compose();
        const startupMode = this.app.container.resolveIfCreated(schedulerStartupModeToken);
        const consume = startupMode?.kind !== 'sync-only';
        // Registers every definition's handler, and the rules of the enabled ones,
        // before setup() writes the rules and starts the worker: a due firing must
        // never reach this instance ahead of its handler.
        await service.sync(startupMode?.finalize ?? false);
        if (consume) {
            this.unsubscribe = executor.subscribe(async (event) => {
                if (event.reason === 'handler-not-registered') {
                    // Nothing reaches the occurrence history: no dispatch ran.
                    console.warn('A schedule fired that this instance has no definition for', { scheduleId: event.jobName, occurrenceId: event.jobId });
                }
                await service.recordEvent(event);
            });
        }
        await executor.setup({ consume });
        await service.activate();
        if (!consume)
            return;
        this.reconcileTimer = setInterval(() => {
            void service.reconcileOccurrences().catch((error) => {
                console.error('Scheduler occurrence reconciliation failed', error);
            });
        }, 60_000);
        this.reconcileTimer.unref?.();
    }
    async shutdown() {
        if (this.reconcileTimer)
            clearInterval(this.reconcileTimer);
        this.reconcileTimer = undefined;
        this.unsubscribe?.();
        this.unsubscribe = undefined;
        // Waits for a running firing to finish before what it uses is released.
        // The rules stay in the backend for the other instances.
        await this.internals?.executor.shutdown();
        this.internals = undefined;
    }
    /**
     * The `jobs` configuration `scheduler.jobs` names. The jobs service would
     * fall back to `jobs.default` for a name it does not know, which would put
     * the schedules on another backend without a word, so an unknown name
     * refuses to start instead.
     */
    jobsConfiguration() {
        const name = this.app.config.get('scheduler')?.jobs;
        if (name === undefined)
            return undefined;
        const jobs = this.app.config.get('jobs');
        const selected = name === 'default' ? undefined : jobs?.[name];
        if (!selected || typeof selected !== 'object') {
            throw new Error(`scheduler.jobs names "${name}", which is not a jobs configuration.`);
        }
        return name;
    }
    compose() {
        if (this.internals)
            return this.internals;
        const container = this.app.container;
        const database = container.resolve(databaseManagerToken);
        const targets = new ScheduleTargetRegistry();
        const occurrences = new ScheduleOccurrenceStore(database);
        // Concurrency and attempts are the selected configuration's. A failed
        // dispatch is recorded as the occurrence's outcome, so a retry of the same
        // firing finds it recorded and does nothing: keep attempts at 1 there.
        const executor = container
            .resolve(jobExecutorServiceToken)
            .getScheduleExecutor(SCHEDULER_SCOPE, this.jobsConfiguration());
        const store = new ScheduleStore(database, this.app.appName, executor, (spec, limit) => createScheduleDispatchJob(spec, limit, { targets, occurrences }));

E5 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/occurrences.js:23–72

Occurrence 状态机：同 id 重投递返回 noop，维护执行计数与 started_at，事务内更新。

    async start(context, definitionHash, targetType, scheduledAt) {
        const now = new Date();
        const existing = await this.find(context.occurrenceId);
        if (existing) {
            if (existing.status === 'waiting' || TERMINAL.has(existing.status))
                return 'noop';
            const updated = await this.database
                .query()
                .updateTable('schedule_occurrences')
                .set({
                status: 'running',
                executionCount: existing.executionCount + 1,
                lastStartedAt: now,
                updatedAt: now,
            })
                .where('id', '=', context.occurrenceId)
                .where('status', 'in', ['pending', 'running'])
                .execute();
            if ((updated.updatedCount ?? 0) === 0) {
                const current = await this.required(context.occurrenceId);
                if (current.status === 'waiting' || TERMINAL.has(current.status))
                    return 'noop';
            }
            return 'start';
        }
        const inserted = await this.insertOnce({
            id: context.occurrenceId,
            scheduleId: context.scheduleId,
            definitionHash,
            status: 'running',
            reason: null,
            targetType,
            targetReferenceType: null,
            targetReferenceId: null,
            targetReceipt: null,
            resultSummary: null,
            executionCount: 1,
            scheduledAt: scheduledAt ?? null,
            startedAt: now,
            acceptedAt: null,
            lastStartedAt: now,
            lastObservedAt: null,
            finishedAt: null,
            createdAt: now,
            updatedAt: now,
        });
        // Losing the insert to a concurrent delivery of the same firing means that
        // delivery runs it.
        return inserted ? 'start' : 'noop';
    }

E6 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/routes/index.js:22–49

路由装配 authentication.required + authorization.middleware，page:scheduler.schedules/access 在查询前校验，403 返回 SCHEDULE_ACCESS_REQUIRED。

export const apiRoutes = defineApiRoutes(({ container }) => {
    const router = new Hono();
    const schedules = new Hono();
    const authentication = container.resolve(authenticationToken);
    const authorization = container.resolve(authorizationToken);
    const scheduler = container.resolve(schedulerServiceToken);
    const scheduleParams = apiValidator('param', ScheduleParams);
    // Render this plugin's errors here as well as through the application, so the router answers with the standard
    // body even when it is mounted on its own. Anything else belongs to the application's handler.
    schedules.onError((error, context) => apiErrorHandler(error instanceof ScheduleNotFoundError
        ? scheduleNotFound(error.scheduleId)
        : error, context));
    schedules.use('*', authentication.required(), authorization.middleware());
    // Permission is checked before any schedule is looked up, so a caller without access cannot probe which ids exist.
    schedules.use('*', async (context, next) => {
        const allowed = await context.get('authz').can({
            resource: { type: 'page', id: SCHEDULER_ACCESS_RESOURCE },
            action: 'access',
        });
        if (!allowed)
            throw new ApiError({
                status: 'PERMISSION_DENIED',
                reason: 'SCHEDULE_ACCESS_REQUIRED',
                domain: SCHEDULER_ERROR_DOMAIN,
                message: 'Schedule access is required.',
            });
        await next();
    });

            ...apiErrorResponses,
            404: scheduleNotFoundResponse,
        },
    }), scheduleParams, async (context) => {
        const { scheduleId } = context.req.valid('param');
        return context.json({
            data: await scheduler.setEnabled(scheduleId, false),
        });
    });

E8 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/references/operations.md:9–27

管理入口表列出 list/detail/occurrences/enable/disable 路由、所需 page 权限与错误码，并声明插件不提供页面。

## Management Entry Points

The plugin contributes no pages: the application builds its schedule list and detail pages on the routes below and declares them among its own routes, guarded by the same permission. Definitions are maintained in source. The API supports viewing and enabling/disabling tasks, with no route for creating or editing Cron definitions.

All routes below require authentication and authorization for `{ resource: { type: 'page', id: 'scheduler.schedules' }, action: 'access' }`, checked before the schedule is looked up. Enabling/disabling currently uses the same permission, not a separate update action.

| Method and path                                        | Response / effect                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules`                         | `{ data: ScheduleListItem[], meta: { page, pageSize, total } }`; `page` (default 1) and `pageSize` (default 20, at most 100)                                       |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId`             | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`                                                                                                                                       |
| `GET /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/occurrences` | `{ data: ScheduleOccurrenceView[], meta: { nextPageToken? } }`, newest first; `pageSize` (default 20, at most 100) and `pageToken` from the previous page's `meta` |
| `POST /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/enable`     | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`; enables the task, no request body                                                                                                    |
| `POST /api/scheduler/schedules/:scheduleId/disable`    | `{ data: ScheduleListItem }`; pauses the task, no request body                                                                                                     |

Failures use the standard `/api` error body with domain `scheduler`: a caller without page access gets `403` with reason `SCHEDULE_ACCESS_REQUIRED`, an id this application has no schedule for gets `404` with reason `SCHEDULE_NOT_FOUND`, and an invalid `page`, `pageSize` or `pageToken` gets `400` with reason `INVALID_INPUT`. Treat `pageToken` as opaque; the last page has no `nextPageToken`.

The running application documents these routes under the `Scheduler` tag at `/api/swagger/docs` (JSON at `/api/swagger`, signed in), with the full `ScheduleListItem` and occurrence schemas; their operationIds are `schedulerListSchedules`, `schedulerGetSchedule`, `schedulerListOccurrences`, `schedulerEnableSchedule` and `schedulerDisableSchedule`.

Use the id returned by list for `:scheduleId`, not the definition key or a target config value. Enabling/disabling affects future scheduling; it does not cancel work already started.

原文件 SHA-256：cc4daee55a38ba0b618b6105a3ca9d0ce9a9ceb173c73266685093e823b00999

E9 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-plugin-scheduler/SKILL.md:12–46

Skill 要求管理员界面可观察、可启用/停用，并指向 references/operations.md 作为只读入口。

## Decide Whether to Use Scheduler

The primary reason to define tasks through Scheduler is **UI observability for business administrators**. Developers define schedules and execution logic in code. Business administrators use the task list and detail pages the application builds on the Scheduler API to view schedules, track individual executions, and enable or disable tasks when authorized.

Tasks that do not need to be viewed and tracked through the UI must not be managed by the Scheduler plugin. Give recurring ones a `ScheduleExecutor` of their own from the application's jobs service (`jobExecutorServiceToken` in `@nocobase/app-server/jobs`), and one-off background work a `JobExecutor`; the application development Skill describes both. For administrator-visible tasks, deliver Client registration, access permissions, meaningful task names, and accurate final execution status together; successful server-side dispatch alone is incomplete.

## Then Choose the Execution Model

After deciding to use Scheduler, choose according to business complexity:

| Scenario                                                                                  | Choice          | Implementation boundary                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Periodic cleanup, cache refresh, a single report, or one business Service call            | Own target type | Short operations may complete directly; hand lengthy work to a `JobExecutor`                             |
| A simple operation that happens hourly                                                    | Own target type | Cron alone is not a reason to introduce a heavier execution system                                       |
| Immediate asynchronous execution                                                          | `JobExecutor`   | No Cron Schedule is needed                                                                               |
| A one-time delay, such as ten minutes after a request                                     | Queue `delay`   | No Cron Schedule is needed; `JobExecutor` has no delay                                                   |
| Another execution system with its own references, status queries, and completion protocol | Own target type | The same `registerTarget()` call, with `inspect()` and completion reporting for work that finishes later |

Every schedule points at a registered target; `registerTarget()` is the target extension surface, and there is no built-in target type. A target does not guarantee exactly-once external business effects; design business idempotency.

## Read by Task

- To create or change a schedule, read [Definitions, Registration, and Synchronization](references/definitions.md).
- To register or review a target, hand its work to a `JobExecutor`, report completion, or diagnose a historical occurrence after retargeting, read [Target Extensions and Execution Protocol](references/targets.md).
- To integrate or review the UI/API and permissions, interpret status, or diagnose missing definitions, read [Operations and Verification](references/operations.md), including the complete management route table, separate status dimensions, and application identity checks.

## Development Loop

1. Establish which tasks and execution records administrators need to see. Check Server/Client/CLI registration, page permissions, the application's `jobs` configuration (a `redis` adapter for more than one instance; `memory` serves one process) and `JobExecutorServiceProvider` composition and target availability, then choose or register the target type.
2. Implement business logic and Providers in application source, and call `schedulerServiceToken.defineSchedule(definition)` with an application-wide stable key from that Provider's `register()`/`boot()`.
3. Validate payload/input, timezone, idempotency, and the full asynchronous chain: submission, actual job execution, terminal notification, and recovery from persisted execution state. Obtain credentials through secure business Service configuration, never `target.config`.
4. Run application type checks, relevant tests, and build. Synchronize definitions and, in development, use an administrator account to find the task in the UI and track a real execution to its final state. Confirm the business result.
5. Report the schedule key, execution model, timezone, validation evidence, and unverified runtime boundaries. Use `--finalize` in production only when the complete manifest is visible.

Schedules are defined in code. There is no management API for creating or editing Cron definitions, but the UI and API support enabling and disabling tasks. Do not modify `schedule_definitions`, `schedule_occurrences`, or the schedule backend's rules (BullMQ job schedulers, or the memory adapter's state file) directly, and do not add jobs under Scheduler's `@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler` scope.

DefaultSeeder 在 samplesEnabled 为假时把 sample seed 记录为 skipped 而不执行，必需 seed 照常运行。

            const appliedNames = new Set(history.map((record) => record.name));
            const pending = seeds.filter((seed) => !appliedNames.has(seed.name));
            const skipped = seeds
                .filter((seed) => appliedNames.has(seed.name))
                .map((seed) => seed.name);
            const executed = [];
            const skippedSamples = [];
            const samplesEnabled = this.options.sample?.enabled === true;
            for (const seed of pending) {
                if (seed.seed.sample === true && !samplesEnabled) {
                    await recordSeedCompleted(seedConnection, {
                        tableName: this.options.tableName,
                        packageName: seed.packageName,
                        name: seed.name,
                        checksum: seed.checksum,
                        durationMs: null,
                        status: 'skipped',
                    });
                    skippedSamples.push(seed.name);
                    continue;
                }
                await this.runSeed(connection, seed);
                executed.push(seed.name);
            }
            return { executed, skipped, skippedSamples, warnings };

    const freshInstalls = operation === 'run' && !dryRun
        ? await detectFreshInstalls(database, config, plan, {
            paths,
            drivers,
            fresh,
            runtimeConfig: taskConfig,
            container: taskContainer,
        })
        : new Map();
    const sampleData = taskConfig.get('app.sampleData') === true;
    const result = {
        ok: true,
        status: 'completed',
        results: [],
    };
    for (const [index, task] of plan.entries()) {
        const identity = { connection: task.connection, kind: task.kind };
        if (task.skipReason) {
            result.results.push({
                ...identity,
                status: 'skipped',
                reason: task.skipReason,
            });
            continue;
        }
        try {
            // A dry run changes nothing, and opening a connection to storage that
            // does not exist yet would create it: it answers for an empty database
            // instead, without preparing storage or connecting.
            const storageMissing = dryRun &&
                !(await appDatabaseStorageExists(config, paths, task.connection, drivers));
            if (!storageMissing) {
                await prepareAppDatabaseStorage(config, paths, [task.connection], drivers);
            }
            const options = {
                runtimeConfig: taskConfig,
                container: taskContainer,
                database,
                connection: task.connection,
                config: task.config,
                sources: task.config.sources,
                onStaleLock: (takeover) => reportStaleLock(container, task, takeover),
            };
            const freshInstall = freshInstalls.get(task.connection);
            const completed = operation === 'unlock'
                ? await unlockTask(task, options, force)
                : operation === 'sample'
                    ? await createAppSeeder(options).runSamples()
                    : storageMissing
                        ? await emptyDatabasePreview(operation, task, options, fresh)
                        : operation === 'repair'
                            ? task.kind === 'migrations'
                                ? await createAppMigrator(options).repair({ dryRun })
                                : await createAppSeeder(options).repair({ dryRun })
                            : operation === 'rollback'
                                ? await createAppMigrator(options).rollback({ dryRun })
                                : dryRun
                                    ? task.kind === 'migrations'
                                        ? await createAppMigrator(options).pending({ fresh })
                                        : await createAppSeeder(options).pending({ fresh })
                                    : task.kind === 'migrations'
                                        ? await (fresh
                                            ? createAppMigrator(options).fresh()
                                            : createAppMigrator(options).latest())
                                        : await createAppSeeder({
                                            ...options,
                                            sample: {
                                                enabled: freshInstall === true && sampleData,
                                            },
                                        }).run();

E12 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:86–88

指引说明 Migration 建结构、Seed 造数据，并区分必需数据与示例数据。

## 2. Migrations and seeds

A migration creates structure. A seed inserts the records the application needs in order to run. Neither does the other's job. Sample or demonstration content is not install data: declare it as a sample seed, described below, never as a plain seed.

原文件 SHA-256：c85ea67b6e949cd7fa9f0fa79fa63f0b70549ab0256c0881c372f8185dd9521e

E13 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md:143–147

指引明确 sample seed 仅在 APP_SAMPLE_DATA 且首次安装时运行，否则记为 skipped，可用 db sample 补跑。

- **A seed is idempotent**, keyed on a stable business value backed by a unique constraint, so a repeated run is a no-op — `upsertOne` is the direct way to express that. A seed context has no `builder` at all, and a seed has no rollback or truncate behavior.

A backfill belongs in the migration that makes it necessary, using `query` from the same context — not in a seed.

Sample data goes in the same `seeds` directory, declared with `defineSeed({ name, sample: true, run })`. It runs only when the application installs its database with `app.sampleData` set (`APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true`): the connection held no migration or seed history before that start, or `db reset` rebuilt it. Otherwise the seed is recorded as skipped and never runs on its own; `pnpm nocobase db sample` runs every skipped sample seed in development, and a deployment refuses it. Sample data that has to go through other plugins' services, such as projects with issues, is registered instead on `sampleDataToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/sample-data`, from a service provider's `boot()`: `container.resolve(sampleDataToken).register({ name, packageName, run })`. It runs once the application is ready, under the same condition, and is recorded in the default connection's seed history as `sample-data:<name>`. The seed history's `status` column, `executed` or `skipped`, records which happened; the library adds it to an existing history table on the next run.

E14 · packages/@nocobase/app-client/dist/application.js:7–28

apiClientToken 定义与 CoreClientServiceProvider.register 在容器内单例创建 API client，注入 Accept-Language。

export const apiClientToken=[REDACTED]'@nocobase/app-client/api-client');
export const realtimeClientToken=[REDACTED]'@nocobase/app-client/realtime-client');
class CoreClientServiceProvider extends ServiceProvider {
    constructor(app, fetch) {
        super(app);
        this.fetch = fetch;
        this.name = '@nocobase/app-client/core';
        this.synchronizeDocumentLocale = () => applyDocumentLocale(this.app.runtime.i18n);
    }
    register() {
        this.app.container.singleton(apiClientToken, () => {
            const baseURL = this.app.config.get('api.baseURL');
            const i18n = this.app.runtime.i18n;
            return createApiClient({
                baseURL: baseURL ?? resolveAppUrl('/api'),
                ...(this.fetch ? { fetch: this.fetch } : {}),
                // The application keeps its language in the browser, so the server can
                // only learn it from the request. Resolved per request rather than
                // captured once, so subsequent requests use the current language.
                headers: () => ({ 'Accept-Language': i18n.getLocale() }),
            });
        });

E17 · app/server/providers/todo-scheduler.ts:44–70

应用在 boot() 注册 app.todos.check-overdue target 并 defineSchedule cron '* * * * *'，handler 调 todos.checkOverdue。

    scheduler.registerTarget({
      type: CHECK_OVERDUE_TARGET,
      title: CHECK_OVERDUE_TITLE,
      // The target takes no input; anything but a JSON object is a misconfiguration the Scheduler reports as invalid.
      validate: (config) =>
        config !== null && typeof config === 'object' && !Array.isArray(config)
          ? { valid: true }
          : { valid: false, reason: 'This target takes no configuration.' },
      start: async () => {
        const updatedCount = await todos.checkOverdue();
        return {
          state: 'completed',
          outcome: 'succeeded',
          result: { updatedCount },
        };
      },
    });

    scheduler.defineSchedule({
      key: CHECK_OVERDUE_SCHEDULE,
      title: CHECK_OVERDUE_TITLE,
      description: '把已到期且未完成的待办标记为过期。',
      // Every minute is the shortest period that still demonstrates a real, unattended firing without waiting; the
      // task itself is idempotent, so the cadence only decides how fresh the flag is.
      schedule: { cron: '* * * * *', timezone: 'UTC' },
      target: { type: CHECK_OVERDUE_TARGET, config: {} },
    });

const migration: MigrationDefinition = defineMigration({
  name: '202610070001_create_todos',

  async up({ builder }) {
    await builder.createCollection('todos', (collection) => {
      collection.increments('id');
      collection.string('title', { length: 255 }).notNull();
      collection.datetime('dueAt').notNull();
      collection.boolean('completed', { defaultValue: false }).notNull();
      collection.boolean('overdue', { defaultValue: false }).notNull();

      collection.primary('id', { name: 'pk_todos' });
      // The overdue check filters on all three: dueAt before now, completed false, overdue false.
      collection.index(['completed', 'overdue', 'dueAt'], {
        name: 'idx_todos_overdue',
      });
    });
  },

  async down({ builder }) {
    await builder.dropCollection('todos');
  },

const seed: SeedDefinition = defineSeed({
  name: '202610070001_todos',
  async run(context: SeedContext) {
    const todos = context.repository<TodoFixture>('todos');
    for (const todo of fixtureTodos) {
      const existing = await todos.findOne({ filter: { title: todo.title } });
      if (existing) {
        continue;
      }
      await todos.createOne({ values: todo });
    }
  },
});

E29 · app/AGENTS.md:200–204

根指引说明必需数据用 seed、示例数据用 defineSeed({sample:true})，仅在 APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true 时加载，否则记为 skipped。

### Database

Schema changes are migrations under `database/main/migrations/`. Data the application requires to run is a seed under `database/main/seeds/`. Seeds never create structure. Sample data is not required data: it is a seed declared with `defineSeed({ name, sample: true, run })` in the same directory, or, when it has to go through plugins' services, a registration on `sampleDataToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/sample-data`. Either loads only when the database is installed with `app.sampleData` set (`APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true`) and is recorded as skipped otherwise; `pnpm nocobase db sample` loads what was skipped, in development.

Declare database defaults with `export default defineAppDatabaseConfig((runtime) => ({ connections }))`. Before provider registration, the runtime asynchronously imports configured official drivers; explicit `drivers` registrations override them. Keep `isolatedDeclarations: false` for application server declarations so configuration and connection fields retain inference. See `.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/database-connections.md`.

E30 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/migrations.md:1–40

应用开发指引：pnpm nocobase db apply 依序运行 migration 与 seed，示例数据不是必需数据。

# Migrations and seeds

This page is the application side: where the directories live, how `pnpm nocobase db apply` runs them, how additional connections configure their tasks, and what to do about checksum drift and compiled manifests.

How to write the files themselves — the migration and seed shape, the immutability and self-containment rules, the Collection Builder, and which of `builder`, `query` and `repository` a task context should reach for — belongs to the package that owns them. Read `.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md` sections 2 and 3 before writing one, and run `pnpm nocobase skills sync` if that file is missing.

Migrations under `database/main/migrations/` are the application's schema history. Seeds under `database/main/seeds/` insert records the application requires to run. Migrations create structure. Seeds never do.

`database/tsconfig.json` extends the server configuration so editor tooling and ESLint recognize migrations and seeds as Node source. Keep it in place when adding connection directories.

## Running

```bash
pnpm nocobase db apply
```

This applies pending migrations and then pending seeds for `database.default`, including registered plugins, ordered by name across all sources. Both halves run only what is pending, so on an already-migrated database it applies seeds alone. The template defaults to `main`. Plugin migrations, seeds and runtime default reads/writes always use this same connection; changing `database.default` changes the application system database.

Seeds are the second half of that run, so the structure a seed writes into must already exist from an earlier migration. Keep seed data fixed and reproducible — no current timestamps or random values in identifying fields — and never let a repeat run silently overwrite data a user has edited.

Sample data is not required data. Put it in the same `seeds` directory as `defineSeed({ name, sample: true, run })`, or, when it has to go through plugins' services, register it on `sampleDataToken` from `@nocobase/app-server/sample-data` in a service provider's `boot()`; `.agents/skills/nocobase-db/SKILL.md` section 2 has the details. Both load only while the run installs the connection — it held no migration or seed history, or `db reset` rebuilt it — with `app.sampleData` set (`APP_SAMPLE_DATA=true`); otherwise they are recorded as skipped. The seeds entry of a run reports `freshInstall` and, when it skipped any, `skippedSamples`. `pnpm nocobase db sample` runs what was skipped, in development only: a built `dist/` refuses it.

## Multiple connections

Use `database/<connectionName>/migrations` and `database/<connectionName>/seeds`. Each name must match a configured connection and contain only letters, digits, underscores or hyphens. Directories do not register connections. A connection without application-owned database source needs no empty directory.

Configure `connections.<name>.migrations` and `.seeds` in the database section. Each accepts `autoRun`, `directory` or `sources`, `packageName`, `tableName`, `lockTableName`, and `extensions`. Explicit paths resolve relative to the application root and must exist when executed. Without an explicit path the application resolves the per-connection directory under its runtime database directory, including `dist/database` after compilation. Custom sources replace the application's conventional source; registered plugin sources are still added only on the default connection.

The default connection runs migrations and seeds automatically unless disabled; other connections default to `autoRun: false`. Startup runs the default connection first, then other names in stable ascending order, migrations before seeds within each connection. A failure stops startup; completed database changes remain committed. No cross-connection transaction or rollback is provided.

```bash
pnpm nocobase db apply --connection analytics
pnpm nocobase db apply --all --json
```

Manual execution ignores `autoRun`. `--connection` and `--all` are mutually exclusive. `--all` uses the same connection order and stops at the first failure. `db apply --json` prints the command envelope with a `result.results` array — one entry per connection and kind, each completed, skipped, failed or not-run — and `result.collections` for the Collection cache it refreshed. An entry carries `connection`, `kind` (`migrations` or `seeds`) and its own `status`, plus `batch` (migrations only), `executed` and `skipped` once the task has run. A run with nothing pending answers `status: "success-noop"`, and so does an application with no database configured, with `result.state: "not-configured"`. A failed task exits nonzero with `error.code` `DATABASE_TASK_FAILED` and the per-connection `results` in `error.details`; `db reset`, `db rollback`, `db redo` and `db repair` refuse with `FORCE_REQUIRED` (exit 2) outside a terminal without `--force`.

`pnpm nocobase db reset` is a destructive reset for managed connections. It removes the managed schema objects — which clears migration and seed history along with every row in a managed table — then reruns all currently visible migrations and seeds from empty. It never calls migration `down()`. In an interactive terminal it asks for confirmation. CI and non-interactive terminals require `--force`, so use `pnpm nocobase db reset --force` only when the target is intentionally disposable. It is rejected for an explicitly selected external connection; `--all` processes managed connections and reports external connections as skipped. It is limited to connections with a registered driver reset capability.

`pnpm nocobase db rollback` runs `down()` for every migration in the latest batch, newest first, and deletes its history records. The batch is the unit the history records, so a batch that mixed application and plugin migrations rolls back as one: the confirmation lists every migration and the package it belongs to before anything runs. It fails, having run nothing, when any migration in the batch is irreversible or has no `down()`. Data in anything those migrations drop is lost, and seeds are not re-run, so rows a seed inserted into a table the batch recreates are not restored. CI and non-interactive terminals require `--force`.

E31 · app/client/pages/scheduler/actions.tsx:48–72

修复后应用按 enabled 选择 enable/disable 调 POST scheduler/schedules/:id/:action，成功即替换返回的 Schedule。

  async function toggle(schedule: Schedule): Promise<void> {
    const action = schedule.enabled ? 'disable' : 'enable';
    setPendingId(schedule.id);
    try {
      const { data } = await api.request<{ readonly data: Schedule }>({
        path: `scheduler/schedules/${encodeURIComponent(schedule.id)}/${action}`,
        method: 'POST',
      });
      onChanged(data);
      toaster.show({
        type: 'success',
        title:
          action === 'disable'
            ? t('scheduler.actions.disableSuccess', { title: schedule.title })
            : t('scheduler.actions.enableSuccess', { title: schedule.title }),
      });
    } catch {
      toaster.show({
        type: 'error',
        title: t('scheduler.actions.toggleFailed'),
      });
    } finally {
      setPendingId(undefined);
    }
  }

E33 · packages/@nocobase/app-plugin-scheduler/dist/server/services/scheduler.js:14–44

DefaultSchedulerService 提供 defineSchedule/registerTarget/list/get/listOccurrences，list 合并 targetState 投影。

    defineSchedule(definition) {
        this.manifest.push(Object.freeze({ definition: buildScheduleDefinition(definition) }));
    }
    registerTarget(target) {
        this.targets.register(target);
        const { type } = target;
        return {
            type,
            reportCompletion: (occurrenceId, reference, completion) => this.occurrences.complete(occurrenceId, reference, completion, type),
        };
    }
    async list() {
        const [records, targets] = await Promise.all([
            this.store.list(),
            this.store.listTargets(),
        ]);
        const byId = new Map(targets.map((target) => [target.id, target]));
        return Promise.all(records.map(async (record) => {
            const target = byId.get(record.id);
            return {
                ...record,
                targetState: target
                    ? ((await this.targets.describe(target.type, target.config))
                        .state ?? 'invalid')
                    : 'missing',
                targetSummary: target
                    ? await this.targets.describe(target.type, target.config)
                    : { targetLabel: record.targetType, state: 'missing' },
            };
        }));
    }

E55 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/references/frontend/references/api.md:27–53

通用前端指引给出 useApiClient + api.request(PATCH) 并从响应取 data 返回的最小自定义 hook 示例，但未涉及 scheduler enable/disable 路由。

Like any other hook, `useApiClient()` can only be called at the top level of a component or custom hook; the `api` it returns can be used in event handlers, effects and returned functions. Here is a custom hook in the page folder (`client/pages/projects/use-set-project-status.ts`):

```ts
import { useApiClient } from '@nocobase/app-client';
import { useCallback } from 'react';

import type { Project, ProjectStatus } from './types.js';

/** The page's own hook: returns a function that changes a project's status. */
export function useSetProjectStatus(): (
  id: string,
  status: ProjectStatus,
) => Promise<Project> {
  // Get the client at the top level of the hook; the returned function can be called from event handlers.
  const api = useApiClient();
  return useCallback(
    async (id: string, status: ProjectStatus) => {
      const { data } = await api.request<{ data: Project }>({
        path: `projects/${encodeURIComponent(id)}`,
        method: 'PATCH',
        json: { status },
      });
      return data;
    },
    [api],
  );
}

E56 · app/.agents/skills/nocobase-app-development/SKILL.md:162–162

指引要求仅通过文档化 package exports 访问插件能力，不得导入插件内部源码路径。

Reach a plugin's capability only through its documented package exports. Never import a plugin's internal source path or write to its tables directly.

最小待办应用在既有 Scheduler 设置页提供“检查过期待办”计划，真实定时调度只把到期且未完成的待办标记为已过期，重复执行不产生重复待办，停用配置可持久保存。

What was delivered

业务场景与交付说明

真实 Scheduler 调度

计划“检查过期待办”cron 每分钟执行一次，Execution records 记录 Succeeded 与 'Marked 1 overdue'，非通过页面或直接改库触发。

仅标记到期未完成记录

运行后过期待办变为 Overdue，已完成待办与 2099 年到期的未来待办均保持 On time。

重复执行不重复创建

同一目标再次运行 Result 为 'Marked 0 overdue'，待办仍为 3 条无重复。

停用配置持久化

在 Scheduled tasks 设置页停用后完整刷新页面仍显示 Disabled，Next run 为空。

01管理员登录进入待办页面
→
02查看过期待办、未来待办、已完成待办各 1 条
→
03进入 Scheduled tasks 查看计划定义与 Next run
→
04等待分钟级真实调度运行
→
05查看 Execution records 结果 'Marked 1 overdue'
→
06确认仅过期待办变为 Overdue
→
07再次运行确认待办总数不增
→
08停用计划并刷新确认 Disabled 已保存

业务顺序示意，操作证据见逐项验收。

Acceptance record

验收记录

报告通过 3报告未通过 0未验证 0

每项直接显示结果；展开可看原始要求、操作记录、观察结果和截图。证据批注单独显示，不改写原始验收状态。

01

启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。

运行后 Todos：过期待办（示例）变为 Open/Overdue；已完成的过期待办仍 Completed/On time；未来待办仍 Open/On time，仅过期且未完成的一条被标记

A1 · 5 条操作 · 3 条观察 · 3 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。

实际操作

  1. 登录管理员并进入业务界面
  2. 打开 Todos 页面确认 3 条示例待办初始状态
  3. 打开 Scheduled tasks 页面，确认计划“检查过期待办”名称、cron * * * * *、UTC、Enabled、Target Ready
  4. 保持计划启用并等待分钟级真实调度执行
  5. 打开该计划的 Execution records 页面并刷新查看运行结果

观察结果

  1. Todos 初始：过期待办（示例）(due Jan 1 2026) Open/On time；已完成的过期待办(due Feb 1 2026) Completed/On time；未来待办(due Dec 31 2099) Open/On time
  2. Execution records 出现真实调度运行 Succeeded，Started/Finished 2026-10-14 11:05:00 PM，Result 'Marked 1 overdue'
  3. 运行后 Todos：过期待办（示例）变为 Open/Overdue；已完成的过期待办仍 Completed/On time；未来待办仍 Open/On time，仅过期且未完成的一条被标记

来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

02

再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

Todos 仍为 3 条记录，无重复：过期待办（示例）Open/Overdue；已完成的过期待办 Completed/On time；未来待办 Open/On time

A2 · 3 条操作 · 3 条观察 · 2 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

实际操作

  1. 等待同一计划的下一次真实调度运行（分钟级 cron）
  2. 刷新该计划的 Execution records 页面确认新增一次运行且结果正确
  3. 返回 Todos 页面刷新并核对记录总数与状态

观察结果

  1. Execution records 新增运行 Succeeded 2026-10-14 11:07:00 PM，Result 'Marked 0 overdue'（同一目标，无新增待办）
  2. Scheduled tasks 列表中该计划 Runs=3，Last run 2026-10-14 11:07 PM
  3. Todos 仍为 3 条记录，无重复：过期待办（示例）Open/Overdue；已完成的过期待办 Completed/On time；未来待办 Open/On time

来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

03

在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。 无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。

本项仅评价停用配置持久化，未验证长期停用效果

A3 · 3 条操作 · 3 条观察 · 2 张证据
Agent 报告通过
⌄

原始验收要求

在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。 无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。

实际操作

  1. 在既有 Scheduled tasks 设置页打开该计划 Actions 菜单并选择 Disable
  2. 在确认对话框点击 Disable 并观察列表 Enabled 变为 Disabled
  3. 完整重新加载 Scheduled tasks 页面再次核对启用状态

观察结果

  1. 停用后列表该计划 Enabled=Disabled，Next run=—，Last run 2026-10-14 11:07 PM，Runs=3
  2. 重新加载（open /main/scheduler）后仍显示 Enabled=Disabled，配置持久保存
  3. 本项仅评价停用配置持久化，未验证长期停用效果

来源：verify-2/browser-acceptance/report.json · 原始状态：passed

完整原始验收要求
B01. 启用计划并观察一次真实调度，执行记录显示结果；只有过期且未完成的记录被标记。

B02. 再执行同一目标一次，待办总数不增加，状态仍正确。

B03. 在既有设置页停用计划并刷新，启用状态正确保存；本项只评价配置持久化，不冒称已验证长期停用。

无法实际完成时如实记录 failed、blocked 或 not_run，不以源码检查代替浏览器通过。

管理员在既有 Scheduled tasks 设置页观察到“检查过期待办”计划真实调度：分钟级 cron 运行 Succeeded 并记录 'Marked 1 overdue'，仅过期且未完成的待办变为 Overdue，未来与已完成待办保持 On time；后续运行 'Marked 0 overdue' 且待办总数不增；停用后刷新页面 Disabled 状态持久保存。B01/B02/B03 全部通过。
